SPOTIFY FOR PODCASTERS — COPY-PASTE MASTER (ALL BOOKS × ALL LANGS)
Author: Picu Bianca-Maria
Generated: 2026-11-09 14:15 UTC
AUTO-REFRESH: python -u scripts/build_spotify_upload_master.py
  (also runs after write_marketing_pack / upsert --refresh-pack)
==============================================================================

HOW TO USE
1. Find your BOOK · LANG section below.
2. Do SHOW SETUP once (name + description + cover file).
3. Upload TRAILER episode first, then Chapter 01 → last, in order.
4. For each field: select between >>> COPY BEGIN and >>> COPY END, Ctrl+C, paste in Spotify.
5. After publish, save live URLs:
     python scripts/upsert_marketing_link.py --book N --lang XX --spotify-show URL --refresh-pack
     python scripts/upsert_marketing_link.py --book N --lang XX --spotify-trailer URL --refresh-pack

RULES
- One show per book × language.
- Audio drama podcast only — ebook stays on Amazon Kindle.
- Show notes always include Gumroad (full audio) + Amazon (ebook) when links exist.
- Explicit = Yes on every episode.

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW NAME
Harbor Light — Bound by Blood (Audio Drama)
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Three locks. One unsigned marriage pact. A Shadow Hand who measures before he speaks.

Elena Voss counts locks, clauses, and the seconds Julian Kane refuses to move. The Obsidian Coven wants her signature, her harbor, and a cost no one names until dawn.

He is not here to negotiate. She is not here to surrender. In Harbor Light mist, loyalty is currency — and love has no line in the ledger.

Harbor Light — dark mafia romance by Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).

>>> COPY END — EPISODE TITLE

>>> COPY BEGIN — EPISODE DESCRIPTION / SHOW NOTES
The Patriarch moved the morning after the session. Not through Corvus—Corvus was filing procedural challenges, which was the move of a man who still believed the institutional machinery was on his side. The Patriarch moved differently.
He moved the way a man moved when he understood that the institutional machinery had turned against him and he needed to act before it completed the turn. Sable's first-floor operation was raided at eight in the morning.

Three locks. One unsigned marriage pact. A Shadow Hand who measures before he speaks.

Elena Voss counts locks, clauses, and the seconds Julian Kane refuses to move. The Obsidian Coven wants her signature, her harbor, and a cost no one names until dawn.

He is not here to negotiate. She is not here to surrender. In Harbor Light mist, loyalty is currency — and love has no line in the ledger.

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Trois verrous. Un pacte de mariage non signé. Une Main d'Ombre qui mesure avant de parler.

Elena Voss compte les verrous, les clauses et les secondes où Julian Kane refuse de bouger. Le Coven d'Obsidienne veut sa signature, son port, et un prix que personne ne nomme avant l'aube.

Il ne négocie pas. Elle ne se rend pas. Dans la brume de Harbor Light, la loyauté est une monnaie — et l'amour n'a pas de ligne au grand livre.

Harbor Light / Lumière du Port — romance mafia sombre par Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).

Tre serrature. Un patto nuziale non firmato. Una Mano d'Ombra che misura prima di parlare.

Elena Voss conta serrature, clausole e i secondi in cui Julian Kane non si muove. Il Coven di Ossidiana vuole la sua firma, il suo porto e un prezzo che nessuno nomina fino all'alba.

Lui non tratta. Lei non si arrende. Nella nebbia di Harbor Light, la lealta e moneta — e l'amore non ha riga nel registro.

Three locks. One unsigned marriage pact. A Shadow Hand who measures before he speaks.

Elena Voss counts locks, clauses, and the seconds Julian Kane refuses to move. The Obsidian Coven wants her signature, her harbor, and a cost no one names until dawn.

He is not here to negotiate. She is not here to surrender. In Harbor Light mist, loyalty is currency — and love has no line in the ledger.

Harbor Light — dark mafia romance by Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).

  (MISSING — do not upload yet)

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW NAME
Hafenlicht, Band 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Drei Schlösser. Ein unsignierter Ehevertrag. Eine Schattenhand, die misst, bevor sie spricht.

Elena Voss zählt Schlösser, Klauseln und die Sekunden, in denen Julian Kane sich nicht bewegt. Der Obsidianische Bund will ihre Unterschrift, ihren Hafen und einen Preis, den niemand vor dem Morgen nennt.

Er verhandelt nicht. Sie kapituliert nicht. Im nebligen Hafenlicht ist Loyalität Währung — und Liebe hat keine Spalte im Hauptbuch.

Harbor Light / Hafenlicht — Dark-Mafia-Romance von Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).
Hier starten — kostenlose Kapitel, Hörbuch & Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/de.html
>>> COPY END — SHOW DESCRIPTION

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW NAME
Lumière du Port, Livre 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Trois verrous. Un pacte de mariage non signé. Une Main d'Ombre qui mesure avant de parler.

Elena Voss compte les verrous, les clauses et les secondes où Julian Kane refuse de bouger. Le Coven d'Obsidienne veut sa signature, son port, et un prix que personne ne nomme avant l'aube.

Il ne négocie pas. Elle ne se rend pas. Dans la brume de Harbor Light, la loyauté est une monnaie — et l'amour n'a pas de ligne au grand livre.

Harbor Light / Lumière du Port — romance mafia sombre par Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).
Commencez ici — chapitres gratuits, audiolivre & Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/fr.html
>>> COPY END — SHOW DESCRIPTION

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW NAME
Luz del Puerto, Libro 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Tres cerrojos. Un pacto matrimonial sin firmar. Una Mano de Sombra que mide antes de hablar.

Elena Voss cuenta cerrojos, clausulas y los segundos en que Julian Kane no se mueve. El Coven de Obsidiana quiere su firma, su puerto y un precio que nadie nombra hasta el amanecer.

El no negocia. Ella no se rinde. En la niebla de Harbor Light, la lealtad es moneda — y el amor no tiene linea en el libro mayor.

Harbor Light / Luz del Puerto — romance mafia oscuro de Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss y Julian Kane).
Empieza aquí — capítulos gratis, audiolibro y Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/es.html

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW NAME
Luce del Porto, Libro 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Tre serrature. Un patto nuziale non firmato. Una Mano d'Ombra che misura prima di parlare.

Elena Voss conta serrature, clausole e i secondi in cui Julian Kane non si muove. Il Coven di Ossidiana vuole la sua firma, il suo porto e un prezzo che nessuno nomina fino all'alba.

Lui non tratta. Lei non si arrende. Nella nebbia di Harbor Light, la lealta e moneta — e l'amore non ha riga nel registro.

Harbor Light / Luce del Porto — romance mafia dark di Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss e Julian Kane).
Inizia qui — capitoli gratis, audiolibro e Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/it.html
>>> COPY END — SHOW DESCRIPTION

Harbor Light, Book 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Three locks. One unsigned marriage pact. A Shadow Hand who measures before he speaks.

Elena Voss counts locks, clauses, and the seconds Julian Kane refuses to move. The Obsidian Coven wants her signature, her harbor, and a cost no one names until dawn.

He is not here to negotiate. She is not here to surrender. In Harbor Light mist, loyalty is currency — and love has no line in the ledger.

Harbor Light — dark mafia romance by Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).
Start here — free chapters, full audiobook & Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/harbor-light.html

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW NAME
Hafenlicht, Band 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Drei Schlösser. Ein unsignierter Ehevertrag. Eine Schattenhand, die misst, bevor sie spricht.

Elena Voss zählt Schlösser, Klauseln und die Sekunden, in denen Julian Kane sich nicht bewegt. Der Obsidianische Bund will ihre Unterschrift, ihren Hafen und einen Preis, den niemand vor dem Morgen nennt.

Er verhandelt nicht. Sie kapituliert nicht. Im nebligen Hafenlicht ist Loyalität Währung — und Liebe hat keine Spalte im Hauptbuch.

Harbor Light / Hafenlicht — Dark-Mafia-Romance von Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).
Hier starten — kostenlose Kapitel, Hörbuch & Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/de.html
>>> COPY END — SHOW DESCRIPTION

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW NAME
Lumière du Port, Livre 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Trois verrous. Un pacte de mariage non signé. Une Main d'Ombre qui mesure avant de parler.

Elena Voss compte les verrous, les clauses et les secondes où Julian Kane refuse de bouger. Le Coven d'Obsidienne veut sa signature, son port, et un prix que personne ne nomme avant l'aube.

Il ne négocie pas. Elle ne se rend pas. Dans la brume de Harbor Light, la loyauté est une monnaie — et l'amour n'a pas de ligne au grand livre.

Harbor Light / Lumière du Port — romance mafia sombre par Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss & Julian Kane).
Commencez ici — chapitres gratuits, audiolivre & Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/fr.html
>>> COPY END — SHOW DESCRIPTION

Luz del Puerto, Libro 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Tres cerrojos. Un pacto matrimonial sin firmar. Una Mano de Sombra que mide antes de hablar.

Elena Voss cuenta cerrojos, clausulas y los segundos en que Julian Kane no se mueve. El Coven de Obsidiana quiere su firma, su puerto y un precio que nadie nombra hasta el amanecer.

El no negocia. Ella no se rinde. En la niebla de Harbor Light, la lealtad es moneda — y el amor no tiene linea en el libro mayor.

Harbor Light / Luz del Puerto — romance mafia oscuro de Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss y Julian Kane).
Empieza aquí — capítulos gratis, audiolibro y Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/es.html

Luce del Porto, Libro 1 — Audio Drama
>>> COPY END — SHOW NAME

>>> COPY BEGIN — SHOW DESCRIPTION
Tre serrature. Un patto nuziale non firmato. Una Mano d'Ombra che misura prima di parlare.

Elena Voss conta serrature, clausole e i secondi in cui Julian Kane non si muove. Il Coven di Ossidiana vuole la sua firma, il suo porto e un prezzo che nessuno nomina fino all'alba.

Lui non tratta. Lei non si arrende. Nella nebbia di Harbor Light, la lealta e moneta — e l'amore non ha riga nel registro.

Harbor Light / Luce del Porto — romance mafia dark di Picu Bianca-Maria (Elena Voss e Julian Kane).
Inizia qui — capitoli gratis, audiolibro e Kindle: https://picubiancamaria.com/it.html
>>> COPY END — SHOW DESCRIPTION
